-<!-- wp:heading -->
-<h2 class="wp-block-heading">Appendix C: The Bridge Rule (Formal Statement)</h2>
-<!-- /wp:heading -->
-
-<!-- wp:paragraph -->
-<p>The following is the explicit abductive bridge rule that links physical organization to phenomenal presence. It is stated openly rather than smuggled in, and it has explicit loss conditions.</p>
-<!-- /wp:paragraph -->
-
-<!-- wp:heading {"level":3} -->
-<h3 class="wp-block-heading">Statement</h3>
-<!-- /wp:heading -->
-
-<!-- wp:paragraph -->
-<p><strong>Bridge Rule (IBE):</strong></p>
-<!-- /wp:paragraph -->
-
-<!-- wp:paragraph -->
-<p><em>If a physical system S exhibits:</em></p>
-<!-- /wp:paragraph -->
-
-<!-- wp:list {"ordered":true} -->
-<ol class="wp-block-list"><!-- wp:list-item -->
-<li><em>Integrated causal dynamics (perturbation at one point propagates in complex, differentiated patterns)</em></li>
-<!-- /wp:list-item -->
-
-<!-- wp:list-item -->
-<li><em>Global availability for flexible control (information is accessible to multiple subsystems for planning, report, and learning)</em></li>
-<!-- /wp:list-item -->
-
-<!-- wp:list-item -->
-<li><em>Stable self-modeling with metacognitive access (system maintains representation of its own states available to higher-order processes)</em></li>
-<!-- /wp:list-item -->
-
-<!-- wp:list-item -->
-<li><em>Graded, lawful degradation under known consciousness-disrupting interventions (anesthesia, sleep, lesions, disorders)</em></li>
-<!-- /wp:list-item --></ol>
-<!-- /wp:list -->
-
-<!-- wp:paragraph -->
-<p><em>Then identifying that organizational regime with phenomenal presence yields greater explanatory compression, predictive accuracy, and intervention guidance than any rival hypothesis that:</em></p>
-<!-- /wp:paragraph -->
-
-<!-- wp:list -->
-<ul class="wp-block-list"><!-- wp:list-item -->
-<li><em>Denies phenomenology (eliminativism)</em></li>
-<!-- /wp:list-item -->
-
-<!-- wp:list-item -->
-<li><em>Reifies phenomenology as a separate substance (dualism)</em></li>
-<!-- /wp:list-item -->
-
-<!-- wp:list-item -->
-<li><em>Takes phenomenology as primitive without mechanism (panpsychism, process primitivism)</em></li>
-<!-- /wp:list-item -->
-
-<!-- wp:list-item -->
-<li><em>Posits phenomenology as "more than" any physical/functional description (the hidden premise)</em></li>
-<!-- /wp:list-item --></ul>
-<!-- /wp:list -->
-
-<!-- wp:heading {"level":3} -->
-<h3 class="wp-block-heading">Status</h3>
-<!-- /wp:heading -->
-
-<!-- wp:paragraph -->
-<p>This is an&nbsp;<strong>abductive identification</strong>, not a deductive proof. It is justified by explanatory power, not logical entailment. It is defeasible: evidence could overturn it.</p>
-<!-- /wp:paragraph -->
-
-<!-- wp:heading {"level":3} -->
-<h3 class="wp-block-heading">Loss Conditions</h3>
-<!-- /wp:heading -->
-
-<!-- wp:paragraph -->
-<p>The bridge rule would be undermined if:</p>
-<!-- /wp:paragraph -->
-
-<!-- wp:list {"ordered":true} -->
-<ol class="wp-block-list"><!-- wp:list-item -->
-<li>Full phenomenology reports persist reliably without the signature set (integration, global availability, self-modeling, graded degradation)</li>
-<!-- /wp:list-item -->
-
-<!-- wp:list-item -->
-<li>The signature set is fully present but phenomenology is reliably absent (not just unreported, but absent)</li>
-<!-- /wp:list-item -->
-
-<!-- wp:list-item -->
-<li>A phenomenal residue variable is instrumentally isolated that varies independently of all organizational variables while having downstream causal effects</li>
-<!-- /wp:list-item -->
-
-<!-- wp:list-item -->
-<li>A competing framework provides greater explanatory compression, predictive accuracy, and intervention guidance</li>
-<!-- /wp:list-item --></ol>
-<!-- /wp:list -->
-
-<!-- wp:heading {"level":3} -->
-<h3 class="wp-block-heading">What This Achieves</h3>
-<!-- /wp:heading -->
-
-<!-- wp:paragraph -->
-<p>The bridge rule makes the abductive step explicit. It does not claim to have "solved" the Hard Problem by deduction. It claims to have:</p>
-<!-- /wp:paragraph -->
-
-<!-- wp:list {"ordered":true} -->
-<ol class="wp-block-list"><!-- wp:list-item -->
-<li>Diagnosed the Hard Problem as depending on an unfalsifiable premise</li>
-<!-- /wp:list-item -->
-
-<!-- wp:list-item -->
-<li>Replaced that premise with a falsifiable alternative</li>
-<!-- /wp:list-item -->
-
-<!-- wp:list-item -->
-<li>Specified loss conditions</li>
-<!-- /wp:list-item -->
-
-<!-- wp:list-item -->
-<li>Earned explanatory standing through predictive and intervention power</li>
-<!-- /wp:list-item --></ol>
-<!-- /wp:list -->
-
-<!-- wp:paragraph -->
-<p>This is what IBE looks like when done honestly in consciousness science.</p>
-<!-- /wp:paragraph -->
-
-<!-- wp:heading {"level":3} -->
-<h3 class="wp-block-heading">Phenomenal Predictions Summary</h3>
-<!-- /wp:heading -->
-
-<!-- wp:paragraph -->
-<p>The following table consolidates the framework's key predictions, mapping consciousness phenomena to organizational mechanisms and falsification conditions:</p>
-<!-- /wp:paragraph -->
-
-<!-- wp:table -->
-<figure class="wp-block-table"><table class="has-fixed-layout"><thead><tr><th>Phenomenon</th><th>Constraint Mechanism</th><th>Falsification Condition</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><strong>Anesthesia abolishes consciousness</strong></td><td>PCI collapse reflects disrupted integrative capacity; constraint closure temporarily broken</td><td>Conscious reportability without PCI elevation</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Sleep reduces consciousness</strong></td><td>Organizational closure persists with attenuated sensory coupling; self-modeling continues with reduced environmental constraint</td><td>Full waking-level phenomenology reports across degraded perturbational complexity</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Death ends consciousness</strong></td><td>Irreversible breakdown of metabolic, neural, and regulatory coupling; no mechanism remains to regenerate closure</td><td>Evidence of experiential remainder after complete closure breakdown</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Locked-in syndrome preserves consciousness</strong></td><td>Internal constraint closure intact; only motor output coupling severed</td><td>High external responsiveness with no internal integrative signature</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Privacy of experience</strong></td><td>Self-model is internal control variable with privileged access; not externally broadcast</td><td>System with full report capacity but public access to intermediate state updates</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Immediacy of experience</strong></td><td>Low-latency availability required for real-time control; self-model doesn't infer its own presence</td><td>Privacy/immediacy reports intact after disrupting privileged integration locus</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Coherence of experience</strong></td><td>Cross-modal unification via global availability; constraint closure prunes incoherent combinations</td><td>Stable unified phenomenology during measurable breakdown of cross-modal synchrony</td></tr><tr><td><strong>"Something it is like" posit</strong></td><td>Globally available integrated control state serves as summary variable for viability tracking</td><td>Absence of phenomenality posit despite all integrative markers present</td></tr></tbody></table></figure>
-<!-- /wp:table -->
-
-<!-- wp:heading {"level":3} -->
-<h3 class="wp-block-heading">Cross-Scale Predictions</h3>
-<!-- /wp:heading -->
-
-<!-- wp:paragraph -->
-<p>The framework is not consciousness-specific. Constraint closure is a general organizational principle that applies across scales. The following predictions test whether the framework generalizes:</p>
-<!-- /wp:paragraph -->
-
-<!-- wp:table -->
-<figure class="wp-block-table"><table class="has-fixed-layout"><thead><tr><th>Scale/Domain</th><th>Prediction</th><th>Falsification Condition</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><strong>Developmental morphology</strong></td><td>Organisms store body plans as distributed constraint networks (bioelectric, biomechanical). Altering these constraints reproducibly redirects growth to coherent alternative forms (as in Levin's planarian and Xenopus work).</td><td>Perturbations to patterning signals produce only random deformities or non-viable tissue, never coherent alternative anatomies</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Collective cognition</strong></td><td>When individuals are richly connected by feedback constraints, higher-level "intelligence" emerges. Ant colonies, bee swarms, and human groups with strong coordination outperform isolated individuals on complex tasks.</td><td>Integrated groups never outperform individuals; coordination always degrades problem-solving</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Convergent evolution</strong></td><td>Independent lineages under similar constraints evolve analogous cognitive features. If consciousness arises from constraint satisfaction rather than special essence, similar constraints should produce similar solutions.</td><td>Hard phylogenetic cutoff where complex cognition appears without precursors; no convergent cognitive features in distant taxa facing similar constraints</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Substrate independence</strong></td><td>Any system achieving recursive self-modeling and integrative constraint closure will exhibit empirical signs of consciousness (novel self-reports, unpredictable self-initiated goals).</td><td>A system with human-level complexity in self-monitoring and integration shows no evidence of phenomenology and only recombines training inputs</td></tr></tbody></table></figure>
-<!-- /wp:table -->
-
-<!-- wp:heading {"level":3} -->
-<h3 class="wp-block-heading">Cross-Scale Constraint Propagation</h3>
-<!-- /wp:heading -->
-
-<!-- wp:paragraph -->
-<p>The framework predicts that interventions at one scale will propagate through organizational closure to other scales in lawful, predictable ways. This is a strong test of the closure hypothesis.</p>
-<!-- /wp:paragraph -->
-
-<!-- wp:paragraph -->
-<p><strong>Molecular → Behavioral → Phenomenal:</strong>&nbsp;Altering ion channel kinetics, receptor densities, or neurotransmitter levels will systematically alter global brain dynamics and conscious states. This is confirmed extensively by pharmacology: anesthetics abolish consciousness by disrupting integrative dynamics; psychedelics alter phenomenal content by modifying serotonergic constraint satisfaction; anxiolytics and antidepressants shift affective constraints by changing molecular parameters. The lawfulness of these mappings (specific molecular targets → specific phenomenal changes) supports the claim that closure propagates constraints across scales.</p>
-<!-- /wp:paragraph -->
-
-<!-- wp:paragraph -->
-<p><strong>Behavioral → Molecular:</strong>&nbsp;Sustained cognitive training, psychological intervention, or traumatic experience will produce measurable changes in gene expression, synaptic density, and connectivity patterns. This is confirmed by neuroplasticity research: meditation produces measurable changes in cortical thickness and connectivity; skill acquisition reshapes motor cortex; trauma alters stress-response circuitry and epigenetic markers. The existence of these top-down effects (behavioral → molecular) demonstrates that constraint propagation is bidirectional.</p>
-<!-- /wp:paragraph -->
-
-<!-- wp:paragraph -->
-<p><strong>Falsification condition for cross-scale propagation:</strong>&nbsp;If molecular interventions produced only local effects with no systematic propagation to behavior or experience (e.g., altering neurotransmitter levels with no behavioral or experiential consequence), or if psychological interventions produced no measurable neural correlates (e.g., years of meditation with zero change in any brain measure), the closure assumption would fail. So far, no such dissociations have been found; instead, every level of intervention produces effects that propagate through the system.</p>
-<!-- /wp:paragraph -->
-

-<!-- wp:heading -->
-<h2 class="wp-block-heading">Appendix E: Assumptions Audit</h2>
-<!-- /wp:heading -->
-
-<!-- wp:paragraph -->
-<p>Every framework in consciousness studies rests on assumptions, many implicit. Making these explicit reveals which are empirical (testable), which are methodological (useful but not claims about the world), and which are metaphysical commitments that cannot be tested. A framework's hidden assumptions are often its most vulnerable points.</p>
-<!-- /wp:paragraph -->
-
-<!-- wp:heading {"level":3} -->
-<h3 class="wp-block-heading">E.1 Ontological Assumptions</h3>
-<!-- /wp:heading -->
-
-<!-- wp:table -->
-<figure class="wp-block-table"><table class="has-fixed-layout"><thead><tr><th>Framework</th><th>Core Assumption</th><th>Type</th><th>Falsifiable?</th><th>Hidden Commitment</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><strong>Constraint-based (this paper)</strong></td><td>Consciousness = organizational closure with self-modeling</td><td>Structural identification</td><td>Yes (5 loss conditions)</td><td>Assumes closure is sufficient, not merely necessary</td></tr><tr><td><strong>IIT</strong></td><td>Consciousness = integrated information (Φ)</td><td>Identity claim</td><td>Partially</td><td>Assumes intrinsic existence from causal structure; panpsychist implications at edges</td></tr><tr><td><strong>GWT</strong></td><td>Consciousness = global broadcast/availability</td><td>Functional claim</td><td>Yes</td><td>Equates access with phenomenology; may miss unreportable experience</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Panpsychism</strong></td><td>Proto-experience pervades matter</td><td>Metaphysical primitive</td><td>No</td><td>Assumes combination is possible without mechanism</td></tr><tr><td><strong>HOT</strong></td><td>Consciousness requires meta-representation</td><td>Architectural claim</td><td>Partially</td><td>Assumes unconscious HOT can confer consciousness on target; regress problem</td></tr><tr><td><strong>FEP</strong></td><td>Systems minimize free energy</td><td>Mathematical identity</td><td>No</td><td>Too broad to fail; any behavior redescribable as free energy minimization</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Orch-OR</strong></td><td>Consciousness requires quantum coherence</td><td>Physical claim</td><td>Yes (timescale)</td><td>Assumes coherence survives decoherence; conflicts with thermal physics</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Process Philosophy</strong></td><td>Experience is fundamental</td><td>Metaphysical primitive</td><td>No</td><td>Cannot specify what would show experience isn't fundamental</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Interface Theory (Hoffman)</strong></td><td>Consciousness is fundamental; spacetime is an interface</td><td>Idealist ontology</td><td>No</td><td>Assumes evolutionary selection presupposes consciousness; parasitic on realist assumptions it claims to replace</td></tr></tbody></table></figure>
-<!-- /wp:table -->
-
-<!-- wp:heading {"level":3} -->
-<h3 class="wp-block-heading">E.2 Epistemological Assumptions</h3>
-<!-- /wp:heading -->
-
-<!-- wp:table -->
-<figure class="wp-block-table"><table class="has-fixed-layout"><thead><tr><th>Framework</th><th>How We Know Consciousness</th><th>Hidden Commitment</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><strong>Constraint-based</strong></td><td>Measurable signatures: integration, perturbational complexity, graded degradation</td><td>Assumes organizational features are accessible to third-person measurement</td></tr><tr><td><strong>IIT</strong></td><td>Compute Φ from causal structure</td><td>Assumes Φ calculation is tractable; in practice relies on proxies</td></tr><tr><td><strong>GWT</strong></td><td>Reportability and behavioral indicators</td><td>Assumes report tracks phenomenology; may miss "overflow"</td></tr><tr><td><strong>HOT</strong></td><td>Presence of higher-order representation</td><td>Assumes metacognitive capacity indicates consciousness; problematic for infants/animals</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Panpsychism</strong></td><td>Inference from own case to all matter</td><td>Assumes analogy is valid despite no shared features with electrons</td></tr><tr><td><strong>FEP</strong></td><td>Model parameters and prediction errors</td><td>Assumes Bayesian quantities map onto subjective quantities</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Interface Theory (Hoffman)</strong></td><td>Mathematical derivation from evolutionary games</td><td>Assumes mathematical structure directly generates phenomenology; confuses description with causation</td></tr></tbody></table></figure>
-<!-- /wp:table -->
-
-<!-- wp:heading {"level":3} -->
-<h3 class="wp-block-heading">E.3 Methodological Assumptions</h3>
-<!-- /wp:heading -->
-
-<!-- wp:table -->
-<figure class="wp-block-table"><table class="has-fixed-layout"><thead><tr><th>Framework</th><th>Research Strategy</th><th>Hidden Commitment</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><strong>Constraint-based</strong></td><td>Identify falsification conditions; test organizational markers</td><td>Assumes falsification-first methodology is appropriate for consciousness</td></tr><tr><td><strong>IIT</strong></td><td>Axiomatic derivation from phenomenology</td><td>Assumes phenomenological axioms are self-evident and complete</td></tr><tr><td><strong>GWT</strong></td><td>Contrastive analysis (conscious vs. unconscious processing)</td><td>Assumes the contrast is exhaustive; no third category</td></tr><tr><td><strong>FEP</strong></td><td>Computational modeling under variational principles</td><td>Assumes brain literally implements Bayesian inference</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Panpsychism</strong></td><td>Conceptual analysis and inference to best explanation</td><td>No experimental methodology; relies on perceived elegance</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Interface Theory (Hoffman)</strong></td><td>Evolutionary game theory and mathematical modeling</td><td>No falsification conditions; any observation reinterpretable as "interface"</td></tr></tbody></table></figure>
-<!-- /wp:table -->
-
-<!-- wp:heading {"level":3} -->
-<h3 class="wp-block-heading">E.4 The Assumption Count</h3>
-<!-- /wp:heading -->
-
-<!-- wp:paragraph -->
-<p>Frameworks can be compared by how many assumptions they require and how testable those assumptions are:</p>
-<!-- /wp:paragraph -->
-
-<!-- wp:table -->
-<figure class="wp-block-table"><table class="has-fixed-layout"><thead><tr><th>Framework</th><th>Ontological Assumptions</th><th>Testable</th><th>Metaphysical Load</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><strong>Constraint-based</strong></td><td>3 (closure, self-modeling, thermodynamic cost)</td><td>All 3</td><td>Low</td></tr><tr><td><strong>GWT</strong></td><td>2 (global broadcast, capacity limits)</td><td>Both</td><td>Low</td></tr><tr><td><strong>IIT</strong></td><td>5 (axioms) + identity claim</td><td>Partially</td><td>Moderate</td></tr><tr><td><strong>HOT</strong></td><td>2 (HOT necessity, can be unconscious)</td><td>Partially</td><td>Moderate</td></tr><tr><td><strong>FEP</strong></td><td>1 (but unfalsifiable)</td><td>No</td><td>Low but empty</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Panpsychism</strong></td><td>2 (ubiquity, combination possible)</td><td>Neither</td><td>High</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Orch-OR</strong></td><td>3 (coherence, objective reduction, microtubules)</td><td>All 3 but 2 falsified</td><td>High</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Process Philosophy</strong></td><td>1 (experience fundamental)</td><td>No</td><td>High</td></tr></tbody></table></figure>
-<!-- /wp:table -->
-
-<!-- wp:paragraph -->
-<p>The constraint-based framework makes explicit, falsifiable assumptions with minimal metaphysical overhead. Competing frameworks either make unfalsifiable assumptions (panpsychism, process philosophy, FEP), make assumptions that have been falsified (Orch-OR), or make assumptions that are partially testable but carry hidden commitments (IIT, HOT).</p>
-<!-- /wp:paragraph -->
-
-<!-- wp:heading {"level":3} -->
-<h3 class="wp-block-heading">E.5 What This Framework Assumes</h3>
-<!-- /wp:heading -->
-
-<!-- wp:paragraph -->
-<p>For transparency, here are the explicit assumptions of the constraint-based framework:</p>
-<!-- /wp:paragraph -->
-
-<!-- wp:list {"ordered":true} -->
-<ol class="wp-block-list"><!-- wp:list-item -->
-<li><strong>Thermodynamic realism</strong>: Physical systems obey Landauer's principle; information processing has energy costs.</li>
-<!-- /wp:list-item -->
-
-<!-- wp:list-item -->
-<li><strong>Organizational closure exists</strong>: Some physical systems exhibit closure of constraints (Montévil-Mossio).</li>
-<!-- /wp:list-item -->
-
-<!-- wp:list-item -->
-<li><strong>Self-modeling is possible</strong>: Some closed systems include variables tracking their own state.</li>
-<!-- /wp:list-item -->
-
-<!-- wp:list-item -->
-<li><strong>Constitution claim</strong>: Systems exhibiting closure with self-modeling constitute the class appropriately described as conscious.</li>
-<!-- /wp:list-item -->
-
-<!-- wp:list-item -->
-<li><strong>Falsifiability commitment</strong>: All claims must specify loss conditions.</li>
-<!-- /wp:list-item --></ol>
-<!-- /wp:list -->
-
-<!-- wp:paragraph -->
-<p><strong>What this framework does NOT assume:</strong></p>
-<!-- /wp:paragraph -->
-
-<!-- wp:list -->
-<ul class="wp-block-list"><!-- wp:list-item -->
-<li>That consciousness is a fundamental property of matter</li>
-<!-- /wp:list-item -->
-
-<!-- wp:list-item -->
-<li>That any particular substrate is required</li>
-<!-- /wp:list-item -->
-
-<!-- wp:list-item -->
-<li>That consciousness is identical to any specific quantity (like Φ)</li>
-<!-- /wp:list-item -->
-
-<!-- wp:list-item -->
-<li>That consciousness requires quantum processes</li>
-<!-- /wp:list-item -->
-
-<!-- wp:list-item -->
-<li>That consciousness is an illusion or merely functional</li>
-<!-- /wp:list-item -->
-
-<!-- wp:list-item -->
-<li>That the Hard Problem has a solution in the traditional sense</li>
-<!-- /wp:list-item --></ul>
-<!-- /wp:list -->
-
-<!-- wp:paragraph -->
-<p>The framework makes a structural identification (consciousness = organizational closure with self-modeling) rather than an identity claim (consciousness = X) or a primitivist claim (consciousness is fundamental). This identification is testable: find systems with closure and self-modeling that lack consciousness signatures, or find consciousness signatures without closure, and the framework fails.</p>
-<!-- /wp:paragraph -->
-
-<!-- wp:separator -->
-<hr class="wp-block-separator has-alpha-channel-opacity"/>
-<!-- /wp:separator -->
-
-<!-- wp:paragraph -->
-<p><em>The framework this essay describes is not complete. It is not final. It is falsifiable. If it survives, that will be evidence that it tracks something real. If it fails, that will be progress. Either way, we will have learned something, which is more than unfalsifiable frameworks can offer.</em></p>
-<!-- /wp:paragraph -->
-
-<!-- wp:paragraph -->
-<p><em>The claim that phenomenology must be "more than" any physical or functional description is not just unfalsifiable. It is anti-scientific in structure, indistinguishable from other immunized metaphysical claims once stripped of anthropocentric sentiment. This framework does not need to defeat that claim on its own terms. It shows that the claim never earned standing in the first place.</em></p>
-<!-- /wp:paragraph -->
-
-<!-- wp:paragraph -->
-<p><em>Survival under falsification pressure is evidence of tracking structure, not proof of final truth. The framework does not claim certainty. It claims testability. That is all any honest inquiry can offer.</em></p>
-<!-- /wp:paragraph -->+
+
+<!-- wp:heading {"level":3} -->
+<h3 class="wp-block-heading">Computation and Formal Models</h3>
+<!-- /wp:heading -->
+
+<!-- wp:paragraph -->
+<p>Turing, A. M. (1937). On computable numbers, with an application to the Entscheidungsproblem. <em>Proceedings of the London Mathematical Society, 2</em>(42), 230–265. <a href="https://doi.org/10.1112/plms/s2-42.1.230" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">DOI: 10.1112/plms/s2-42.1.230</a>.</p>

-<p>A stronger interpretation proposes that biological structures causally access an independently existing Platonic morphospace. When that is offered as an explanation for a physical result, it needs a bridge. What property of the alleged ingression interface is measured? Which intervention or prediction distinguishes access to a pre-existing nonphysical form from a well-specified dynamical, developmental, or organizational account? The <a href="https://sweetrationalism.com/articles/biology/bioelectricity-morphogenesis/michael-levin-biological-platonism-chladni-plate-challenge/">Chladni Plate Challenge to Michael Levin’s Biological Platonism</a> develops a related dynamical alternative.</p>
+<p>A stronger interpretation proposes that biological structures causally access an independently existing Platonic morphospace. When that is offered as an explanation for a physical result, it needs a bridge. What property of the alleged ingression interface is measured? Which intervention or prediction distinguishes access to a pre-existing nonphysical form from a well-specified dynamical, developmental, or organizational account? The <a href="https://sweetrationalism.com/articles/biology/bioelectricity-morphogenesis/michael-levin-biological-platonism-chladni-plate-challenge/">Chladni Plate Challenge to Michael Levin’s Biological Platonism</a> develops a related dynamical alternative (see the <a href="https://sweetrationalism.com/articles/biology/michael-levin-science-or-pseudoscience-criticism-index/">Michael Levin evidence and scholarly criticism index</a> for the complete research collection).</p>
